###deimos_expertmode
# @name: TTS Side-Worlds
# @zone: Wizard City
# @author: KamarJ
# @format: Expertmode
# @clients: > 1
# @description: A complete auto quester for the entirety of Side-Worlds. Combat/Dialogue/Accept Side Quests should be enabled. Un-equip all homes before running.

# -----[ Settings ]----- #
var DebugMode = True     # Will show exact steps more in depth. Useful for determining breaks.
var RebuyPotions = True     # Will attempt to refill potions if you're empty.
var Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True     # Will attempt to resume dungeons upon dieing.
var Recconect_To_Game = True     # If you disconnect, will attempt to recconect.
var Train_Spells = False     # Will attempt to train all learnable spells at levels 25, 35, 50, 75, 100, 135, 150, 170 (Only goes to the schools teacher. Does not complete spell quests.)
var Sell_Inventory_At_Max_Bag = False     # Will quick sell once anyone (besides main) reaches 150 bag size. Lock items you don't want sold.
var Auto_Find_Quest = True     # After roughly 5 loops, will attempt to find your lost quest (only if it's actually lost)

# WARNING: If the script ever restarts or crashes, this will attempt to create another adventure party. 
var Create_Adventure_Party = False

var All_Accounts_Are_Questing = False     # Set to true if you do not have a booster and all accounts are questing.

# If you're solo questing, you do not have to change any of the account information below EXCEPT Main_Account_School IF you have 'Train_Spells' enabled.
var Main_Account = "QuestingAccountName"     # Name of the main account. Also MUST BE SET AS p1. Ensure all account names are wrapped with quotes. Example: ("LeftClick Simulator")
var Main_Account_School = "SchoolGoesHere"     # (e.g: Life, Storm, Death, Balance, Ice, Fire, Myth)

var Questee2 = "QuestingAccountName"     # Name of a questing account
var Questee2_School = "SchoolGoesHere"

var Questee3 = "QuestingAccountName"     # ONLY CHANGE if you have a third questing account (Same for the following questing accounts)
var Questee3_School = "SchoolGoesHere"

var Questee4 = "QuestingAccountName"     # 4th questing account
var Questee4_School = "SchoolGoesHere"

var SpeedDelay = 1     # How long each step of the bot will take in seconds.
var DialogDelay = 1     # How long before the bot proceeds after dialogue. (If you're not picking up quests, set it longer.)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

# -----[ DO NOT TOUCH ] ----- #
var Level25 = False
var Level35 = False
var Level50 = False
var Level75 = False
var Level100 = False
var Level135 = False
var Level150 = False
var Level170 = False
var Path_To_Use = ['WorldView', '', 'messageBoxBG', 'ControlSprite', 'optionWindow', 'wbtnWizardCity']
var Created_Party = False
var WatchdogState = 0     # Tracks how many loop iterations without a zone change
var QuestCheckState = 0     # Tracks how many loop iterations before quest check triggers
var P1_Retrieve_Mainline_Quest = False
var P2_Retrieve_Mainline_Quest = False
var P3_Retrieve_Mainline_Quest = False
var P4_Retrieve_Mainline_Quest = False
# --------------------------- #
block Close_Menus {
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Force closing any possible open UI..."
	}
	#_____________________________________#
	# Possible windows that could open up 
	#_____________________________________#
	clickwindow ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'wndDialogMain', 'Exit']
	clickwindow ['WorldView', 'ShoppingReagentWindow', 'buyWindow', 'exit']
	clickwindow ['WorldView', '', 'Exit']
	clickwindow ['WorldView', 'TournamentRanking', 'exit']
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'mainwindow', 'exit' ]
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'main', 'exit' ]
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'exit' ]
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'NPCTrainingGUI', 'Dialog', 'ExitButton' ]
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'NPCTrainingGUI', 'TrainingSelection', 'ExitButton' ]
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'ShoppingPetSnackWindow', 'buyWindow', 'exit' ]
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'wndDialogMain', 'btnYes' ]
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'wndDialogMain', 'btnRight' ]
	clickwindow [ 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton' ]
	clickwindow ['MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton']
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton' ]
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton' ]
	clickwindow ['WorldView', '', 'messageBoxBG', 'ControlSprite', 'teleportButton']
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PetLevelUpWindow', 'wndPetLevelBkg', 'btnPetLevelClose' ]
	clickwindow ['WorldView', 'ClassPicture', 'exit']
	clickwindow ['WorldView', 'HelpHousingTips2', 'toolbar', 'exit']
	if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndCharacter', 'FriendsSinceLabel'] {
		sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'NewFriendsListWindow', 'btnClose']
	}
	clickwindow ['WorldView', 'SocialSystemsWindow', 'CloseSocialSystemsButton']
	clickwindow [ 'WorldView', '', 'messageBoxBG', 'ControlSprite', 'cancelButton' ]
	if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'QuitButton'] and any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'IdleButton'] {
		sameany sendkey ESC, .1
	}
	if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowQuickChat', 'btnRadialQuickChat', 'ControlButton26'] {
		sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowQuickChat', 'btnRadialQuickChat'] 
	}
	clickwindow ['WorldView', 'NPCTrainingShadowMagicGUI', 'Dialog', 'ExitButton']
	clickwindow ['MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'RetryBtn']
	#_____________________________________#
}
block Clear_Dialogue {
	call Close_Menus
	times 3 {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking if dialogue appeared..."
		}
		times 30 {
			if any hasdialogue {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Dialogue detected. Clearing..."
				}
				if Handle_Dialogue = True {
					sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
		sleep $DialogDelay
	}
}
block Load {
    print "Loading zone, waiting for quick chat button..."
    sleep 3
    times 1200 {
        if windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowQuickChat', 'btnRadialQuickChat'] {
            cursorwindow ['WorldView', 'windowQuickChat', 'btnRadialQuickChat']
            if windowtext ['ToolTipContainer', 'ToolTipText'] "right-click on a phrase to add to/remove from favorites" {
                break
            } 
        } 
        sleep .1
    }
}
block Auto_Adventure_Party {
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Auto Adventure Party initiated..."
	}
	if except p1 level > 29 {
		if playercount 2 {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print $Questee2
				print " is adding booster to group..."
			}
			p2 selectfriend $Main_Account
			sleep 1
			p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndCharacter', 'ButtonLayout', 'btnInvite']
			times 30 {
				if any windowvisible ['PartyInviteWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton'] {
					sameany clickwindow ['PartyInviteWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					break
				}
				sleep .1
			}
			p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'SocialSystemsWindow', 'CloseSocialSystemsButton']
		} elif playercount 3 {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print $Questee3
				print " is adding booster to group..."
			}
			p3 selectfriend $Main_Account
			sleep 1
			p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndCharacter', 'ButtonLayout', 'btnInvite']
			times 30 {
				if any windowvisible ['PartyInviteWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton'] {
					sameany clickwindow ['PartyInviteWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					break
				}
				sleep .1
			}
			p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'SocialSystemsWindow', 'CloseSocialSystemsButton']
		} elif playercount 4 {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print $Questee4
				print " is adding booster to group..."
			}
			p4 selectfriend $Main_Account
			sleep 1
			p4 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndCharacter', 'ButtonLayout', 'btnInvite']
			times 30 {
				if any windowvisible ['PartyInviteWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton'] {
					sameany clickwindow ['PartyInviteWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					break
				}
				sleep .1
			}
			p4 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'SocialSystemsWindow', 'CloseSocialSystemsButton']
		}
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Invite sent. Creating Adventure Party..."
		}
		times 3 {
			call Close_Menus
		}
		if playercount 2 {
			p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'SocialSystemsContainer']
			sleep 2
			p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'SocialSystemsWindow', 'LeftButtonLayout', 'CreateAdventurePartyButton'] 
			sleep 2
			p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
			sleep 2
			p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'AdventurePartyNameModalWindow', 'Base', 'ControlSprite', 'DoneButton']
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Adventure Party created. Handling post-creation dialogs..."
			}
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton'] {
				sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
			}  
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton'] {
				sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton']
			}
		} elif playercount 3 {
			p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'SocialSystemsContainer']
			sleep 2
			p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'SocialSystemsWindow', 'LeftButtonLayout', 'CreateAdventurePartyButton'] 
			sleep 2
			p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
			sleep 2
			p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'AdventurePartyNameModalWindow', 'Base', 'ControlSprite', 'DoneButton']
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Adventure Party created. Handling post-creation dialogs..."
			}
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton'] {
				sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
			}  
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton'] {
				sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton']
			}
		} elif playercount 4 {
			p4 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'SocialSystemsContainer']
			sleep 2
			p4 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'SocialSystemsWindow', 'LeftButtonLayout', 'CreateAdventurePartyButton'] 
			sleep 2
			p4 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
			sleep 2
			p4 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'AdventurePartyNameModalWindow', 'Base', 'ControlSprite', 'DoneButton']
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Adventure Party created. Handling post-creation dialogs..."
			}
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton'] {
				sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
			}  
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton'] {
				sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton']
			}
		}
	}
}
block First_Run_Settings {
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Auto configuring settings..."
	}
	if NOT p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'OpenCantripsButton'] {
		print "P1 does not have cantrips unlocked. For full AFK, please complete cantrips 101 given by Aber K Doodle in the commons."
	}
	if NOT p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'OpenCantripsButton'] {
		print "P2 does not have cantrips unlocked. For full AFK, please complete cantrips 101 given by Aber K Doodle in the commons."
	}
	if NOT p3 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'OpenCantripsButton'] {
		print "P3 does not have cantrips unlocked. For full AFK, please complete cantrips 101 given by Aber K Doodle in the commons."
	}
	if NOT p4 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'OpenCantripsButton'] {
		print "P4 does not have cantrips unlocked. For full AFK, please complete cantrips 101 given by Aber K Doodle in the commons."
	}
	times 4 {
		times 300 {
			if NOT any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'QuitButton'] {
				sameany sendkey ESC, .1
			} else {
				break
			}
			sleep .1 
		}
	}
	times 2 {
		clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'TabWindow', 'VideoTab']
	}
	times 100 {
		if NOT p1 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionResolution', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>800x600" {
			p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionResolution', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p2 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionResolution', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>800x600" {
			p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionResolution', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p3 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionResolution', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>800x600" {
			p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionResolution', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p4 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionResolution', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>800x600" {
			p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionResolution', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionResolution', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>800x600" {
			break
		}
	}
	times 100 {
		if NOT p1 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionFullscreen', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>off" {
			p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionFullscreen', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p2 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionFullscreen', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>off" {
			p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionFullscreen', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p3 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionFullscreen', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>off" {
			p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionFullscreen', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p4 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionFullscreen', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>off" {
			p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionFullscreen', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'VideoOptions', 'OptionFullscreen', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>off" {
			break
		}
	}
	times 2 {
		clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'TabWindow', 'GameplayTab']
	}
	times 100 {
		if NOT p1 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'GameplayOptions', 'OptionChatMenu', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>both" {
			p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'GameplayOptions', 'OptionChatMenu', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p2 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'GameplayOptions', 'OptionChatMenu', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>both" {
			p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'GameplayOptions', 'OptionChatMenu', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p3 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'GameplayOptions', 'OptionChatMenu', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>both" {
			p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'GameplayOptions', 'OptionChatMenu', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p4 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'GameplayOptions', 'OptionChatMenu', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>both" {
			p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'GameplayOptions', 'OptionChatMenu', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'GameplayOptions', 'OptionChatMenu', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>both" {
			break
		}
	}
	times 2 {
		clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions' ]
	}
	times 100 {
		if NOT p1 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuestFinder', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>on" {
			p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuestFinder', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p2 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuestFinder', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>on" {
			p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuestFinder', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p3 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuestFinder', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>on" {
			p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuestFinder', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p4 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuestFinder', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>on" {
			p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuestFinder', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AdvGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuestFinder', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>on" {
			break
		}
	}
	times 2 {
		clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'TabWindow', 'MoreGameplayTab']
	}
	times 100 {
		if NOT p1 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionRememberRealm', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>yes" {
			p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionRememberRealm', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p2 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionRememberRealm', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>yes" {
			p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionRememberRealm', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p3 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionRememberRealm', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>yes" {
			p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionRememberRealm', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p4 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionRememberRealm', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>yes" {
			p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionRememberRealm', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionRememberRealm', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>yes" {
			break
		}
	}
	times 100 {
		if NOT p1 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionEndorsements', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>no" {
			p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionEndorsements', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p2 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionEndorsements', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>no" {
			p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionEndorsements', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p3 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionEndorsements', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>no" {
			p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionEndorsements', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}

			p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'MoreGameplayOptions', 'OptionEndorsements', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}

			break
		}
	}
	times 2 {
		clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'TabWindow', 'AnotherGameplayButton' ]
	}
	times 100 {
		if NOT p1 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AnotherGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuickFriendTeleport', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>no" {
			p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AnotherGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuickFriendTeleport', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if NOT p2 windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AnotherGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuickFriendTeleport', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>no" {
			p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AnotherGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuickFriendTeleport', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}

			p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AnotherGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuickFriendTeleport', 'OptionControl', 'OptionButtonLeft' ]
		}
		if windowtext [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'AnotherGameplayOptions', 'OptionQuickFriendTeleport', 'OptionControl', 'OptionValue' ] "<center>no" {
			break
		}
	}
	times 2 {
		clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'OkButton' ]
	}
	print "Auto configuration complete. Happy questing!"
}
block Retrieve_Lost_Quests {
	block Enter_Sigil {
		times 5 {
			if NOT any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground', 'TeamUpButton'] {
				sameany minustp XYZ(0,0,1000)
				sameany plustp XYZ(0,0,1000)
				sleep 2
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
		times 30 {
			sendkey X, .1
		}
		sleep 10
		call Load
	}
	block Click_Quest_Finder {
		times 100 {
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo0', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'txtQuestFinder'] {
				if sameany windowtext ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo0', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'txtQuestFinder'] "<center>find an available quest nearby</center>" {
					times 2 {

block Check_Active_Mainline_Quest {
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Checking if we have an active mainline quest..."
	}
	call Close_Menus
	if playercount 1 {
		times 100 {
			if NOT p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'QuestLogMainlineAndSideButton'] {
				p1 sendkey Q, .1
			} else {
				break
			} 
		}
		times 2 {
			p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'QuestLogAllButton']
		}
		times 30 {
			if p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo0', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline'] {
				p1 cursorwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo0', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline']
				times 30 {
					if p1 windowtext ['ToolTipContainer', 'ToolTipText'] "main quest" {
						print "Mainline quest is active. Continuing..."
						var P1_Retrieve_Mainline_Quest = False
						#p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo0', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'txtQuestFinder']
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
				break
			} elif p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo1', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline'] {
				p1 cursorwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo1', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline']
				times 30 {
					if p1 windowtext ['ToolTipContainer', 'ToolTipText'] "main quest" {
						print "Mainline quest is active. Continuing..."
						var P1_Retrieve_Mainline_Quest = False
						#p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo1', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'txtQuestFinder']
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
				break
			} elif p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo2', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline'] {
				p1 cursorwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo2', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline']
				times 30 {
					if p1 windowtext ['ToolTipContainer', 'ToolTipText'] "main quest" {
						print "Mainline quest is active. Continuing..."
						var P1_Retrieve_Mainline_Quest = False
						#p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo2', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'txtQuestFinder']
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
				break
			} elif p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo3', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline'] {
				p1 cursorwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo3', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline']
				times 30 {
					if p1 windowtext ['ToolTipContainer', 'ToolTipText'] "main quest" {
						print "Mainline quest is active. Continuing..."
						var P1_Retrieve_Mainline_Quest = False
						#p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo3', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'txtQuestFinder']
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
				break
			}
			p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'btnPrevPage']
		}
		if NOT p1 windowtext ['ToolTipContainer', 'ToolTipText'] "main quest" { 
			print "P1 main story quest not active."
			var P1_Retrieve_Mainline_Quest = True
		}

			} else {

block Quest_Check_Watchdog {
	if Auto_Find_Quest = True {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Quest_Check: Iteration counter advanced..."
		}
		if QuestCheckState = 0 {
			var QuestCheckState = 1
			return
		}
		if QuestCheckState = 1 {
			var QuestCheckState = 2
			return
		}
		if QuestCheckState = 2 {
			var QuestCheckState = 3
			return
		}
		if QuestCheckState = 3 {
			var QuestCheckState = 4
			return
		}
		if QuestCheckState = 4 {
			print "QUEST_CHECK TRIGGERED: Verifying mainline quest is still active..."
			call Check_Active_Mainline_Quest
			var QuestCheckState = 0
			print "Quest_Check: Verification complete. Resuming normal operation."
			return
		}
	}
}
block Sell_Inventory {
	if Sell_Inventory_At_Max_Bag = True {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking bag sizes..."
		}
		if playercount > 1 {
			if except p1 bagcount > 149 {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Questee bag is full. Initiating quick sell..."
				}
				times 2 {
					tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
				}
				call Close_Menus
				times 100 {
					if NOT except p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'InventorySpellbookPage', 'QuickSell_Item'] {
						except p1 sendkey B, .1
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1 
				}
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Quick sell window opened. Selling all categories..."
				}
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'InventorySpellbookPage', 'QuickSell_Item']
				sleep 2
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Hat']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Robe']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Shoes']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Weapon'] 
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Athame']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Amulet']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Ring']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Pet']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Deck']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'sellAction']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'QuickSellConfirmationWindow', 'SellButton']
				sleep 1
				except p1 clickwindow ['MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']  
				sleep 3
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Quick sell complete. Closing inventory..."
				}
				except p1 sendkey B, .1
			}
		} else {
			if bagcount > 149 {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Bag is full (solo). Initiating quick sell..."
				}
				times 2 {
					tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
				}
				call Close_Menus
				times 100 {
					if NOT windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'InventorySpellbookPage', 'QuickSell_Item'] {
						sendkey B, .1
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1 
				}
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Quick sell window opened (solo). Selling all categories..."
				}
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'InventorySpellbookPage', 'QuickSell_Item']
				sleep 2
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Hat']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Robe']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Shoes']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Weapon'] 
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Athame']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Amulet']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'BackpackStuff', 'ShopCategory_Ring']
				sleep 1
				clickwindow ['WorldView', 'shopGUI', 'buyWindow', 'AllAction']
				sleep 1

	}

block Spell_Learner {
	block Find_Train_Option {
		block Any_Dialogue {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Clearing dialog for any players with dialogue..."
			}
			if any hasdialogue {
				times 30 {
					#call Close_Menus
					sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
		}
		if NOT any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCTrainingGUI', 'TrainingSelection', 'TrainButton'] {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Train button not visible. Trying different menu positions..."
			}
			times 20 {
				sameany click 533, 286
			}
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Attempt 1: Clicking 426,277..."
			}
			sameany click 426, 277
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCTrainingGUI', 'TrainingSelection', 'TrainButton'] {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Train button found after attempt 1!"
				}
				return
			}
			call Any_Dialogue
			sleep 1
			call Any_Dialogue
			times 10 {
				sameany sendkey X, .1
			}
			#----#
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Attempt 2: Clicking 418,232..."
			}
			sameany click 418, 232
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCTrainingGUI', 'TrainingSelection', 'TrainButton'] {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Train button found after attempt 2!"
				}
				return
			}
			call Any_Dialogue
			sleep 1
			call Any_Dialogue
			times 10 {
				sameany sendkey X, .1
			}
			#----#
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Attempt 3: Clicking 416,189..."
			}
			sameany click 416, 189
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCTrainingGUI', 'TrainingSelection', 'TrainButton'] {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Train button found after attempt 3!"

			}

				sameany sendkey X, .1
			}

			if DebugMode = True {

			sleep 2

				}
				return
			}

			}
			#----#
			if DebugMode = True {

			}
			sameany click 418, 104
			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCTrainingGUI', 'TrainingSelection', 'TrainButton'] {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "Train button found after attempt 5!"
				}
				return
			}

			times 10 {

			}

			sleep 2
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCTrainingGUI', 'TrainingSelection', 'TrainButton'] {

	}
	block Learn_All_Spells {

	block Go_To_Ravenwood {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Marking location..."
		}
		clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'MarkButton' ]	
		sleep $SpeedDelay
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Going to dorm room..."
		}
		until windowdisabled [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'GotoDormButton' ] {
			clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'GotoDormButton' ]
		}
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Waiting for zone to change..."
		}
		waitforzonechange completion
		sleep 3
		if inzone WizardCity/Interiors/WC_Housing_Dorm_Interior {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Forcing characters to dorm room door..."
			}
			until hasxyz XYZ(-202.81295776367188, 22.34906578063965, 0.0) {
				tp XYZ(-202.81295776367188, 22.34906578063965, 0.0)
			}
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Waiting for zone to change..."
			}
			waitforzonechange completion
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Manually waiting 5 additional seconds before resuming..."
			}
			sleep 5
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Currently in ravenwood."
			}
		} else {
			print "Not inside dorm. Un-equip all homes and restart bot."
			restart
		}
	}
	block Go_To_Arcanum {
		call Go_To_Ravenwood
		tp XYZ(10.837, 1919.342, 76.942)
		waitforzonechange completion
		sleep 3
		entitytp nav UniverseTeleport
		sleep 2
		times 30 {
			sendkey X, .1
		}
		if any level > 105 {
			times 4 {     # Up to 4 players
				times 7 {     # Up to 7 world door pages
					if any windowvisible ['WorldView', '', 'messageBoxBG', 'ControlSprite', 'optionWindow', 'wbtnArcanum'] {
						sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', '', 'messageBoxBG', 'ControlSprite', 'optionWindow', 'wbtnArcanum']
						break
					} else {
						clickwindow ['WorldView', '', 'messageBoxBG', 'ControlSprite', 'optionWindow', 'rightButton']
					}
				}
			}
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', '', 'messageBoxBG', 'ControlSprite', 'optionWindow', 'wbtnArcanum'] {
				if sameany windowvisible ['WorldView', '', 'messageBoxBG', 'ControlSprite', 'teleportButton'] {
					sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', '', 'messageBoxBG', 'ControlSprite', 'teleportButton']
					sameany waitforzonechange completion
					sleep 3
					sameany tp XYZ(-3.426, -7499.881, -106.124)
				} 
			}
		} else {
			return
		}
	}
	if Train_Spells = True {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking if questee's are at a certain level..."
		}

			if Level25 = False {
				var Level25 = True
			}
		}
		if any level 35 {

				var Level35 = True
			}
		}
		if any level 50 {

			}
		}
		var ShouldTrainArcanum = False

				var Level135 = True
			}

						sleep 3

						times 2 {

block Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2 {
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "P1 going to P2..."
	}
	call Close_Menus
	p1 friendtp $Questee2
	times 6 {	
		if p1 windowvisible ['MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton'] {
			print "Unable to teleport. Maybe a solo area?" 																				
			p1 clickwindow ['MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'rightButton']   
			return
		} else {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Waiting for potential Teleport fail..."
			}
			sleep 1
		}
	}
	if NOT p1:p2 samezone {				
		times 1000 {
			if NOT p1:p2 sameplace {
				sleep .1
			} else {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P1 and P2 are in the sameplace. Continuing..."
				}
				break
			}
		}
		sleep 2							
	} else {							
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "P1 and P2 are in the same zone. Continuing..."
		}
		sleep 6							
	}     								
}

block Return_To_Booster_P2 {
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "P2 returning to P1..."
	}
	sleep 3
	times 15 {
		if p2 loading {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "P2 is loading. Waiting..."
			}
			sleep 2
		} else {
			break
		}
	}
	sleep 3
	if p1:p2 samezone {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "P2 going to P1."
		}
		p2 tp p1
	} else {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "P2 is in a different zone. Teleporting to P1..."
		}
		p2 friendtp Main_Account
	}
}

block Dungeon_Entrance {
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Dungeon detected. Pressing X on all clients..."
	}
	times 20 {
		sendkey X, .1
	}
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Waiting 15 seconds..."
	}
	sleep 15
}
block Buy_Potions_If_Needed {
	if RebuyPotions = True {
		if any potioncountbelow 1 {
			if NOT sameany incombat {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print anyclientlist
					print "Marking location..."
				}
				sameany clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'MarkButton' ]	
				sleep $SpeedDelay
				if DebugMode = True {
					print anyclientlist
					print "Going to dorm room..."
				}
				until sameany windowdisabled [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'GotoDormButton' ] {
					sameany clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'GotoDormButton' ]
				}
				if DebugMode = True {
					print anyclientlist
					print "Waiting for zone to change..."
				}
				call Load
				sleep 2
				if sameany inzone WizardCity/Interiors/WC_Housing_Dorm_Interior {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Forcing characters to dorm room door..."
					}
					until sameany hasxyz XYZ(-202.81295776367188, 22.34906578063965, 0.0) {
						sameany tp XYZ(-202.81295776367188, 22.34906578063965, 0.0)
					}
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Waiting for zone to change..."
					}
					call Load
					sleep 2
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Traveling to hilda..."
					}
					until sameany hasxyz XYZ(-2.2481577396392822, -2181.48193359375, -153.10992431640625) { 
						sameany tp XYZ(-2.2481577396392822, -2181.48193359375, -153.10992431640625)
					}
					call Load
					sleep 2
					until sameany hasxyz XYZ(-4370.94384765625, 1067.6156005859375, 229.00079345703125) { 
						sameany tp XYZ(-4370.94384765625, 1067.6156005859375, 229.00079345703125)
					}
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Opening potion vendor page..."
					}
					until sameany windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'main', 'fillallpotions' ] {
						sameany sendkey X, .1
					}
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Clicking fill all potions..."
					}
					times 2 {
						sameany clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'main', 'fillallpotions' ]
					}
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						sameany print gold
						print "Attempting to purchase potions..."
					}
					sameany clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'main', 'buyAction' ]
					sleep $SpeedDelay
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Using 1 potion..."
					}
					sameany usepotion
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Clicking refill potions again..."
					}
					times 2 {
						sameany clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'main', 'fillallpotions' ]
					}
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Attempting to purchase again..."
					}
					sameany clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'main', 'buyAction' ]
					sleep $SpeedDelay
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Exiting potion vendor menu..."
					}
					until NOT sameany windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'main', 'exit' ] {
						sameany clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'main', 'exit' ]
					}
					if DebugMode = True {
						print anyclientlist
						print "Returning by recall OR returning to dungeon instance..."
					}
					if sameany windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
						sameany clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
						return
					} 
					sameany clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'RecallButton' ]
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

block Waiting_For_All_To_Arrive {
	print "Waiting for all clients to reach this sigil."
}
block Dungeon_X_Key_Mass {
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Pressing X on all clients..."
	}
	times 20 {
		sendkey X, .1
	}
	call Clear_Dialogue
}
block Dungeon_Combat_Handler_All_Clients {
	block Quick_Refresh {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Attempting quick restore of health/mana for all clients."
		}
		times 4 {
			entitytp health
			entitytp mana
		}
	}
	times 7 {
		if incombat {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "All clients are in combat. Waiting..."
			}
			waitforcombat completion
			call Quick_Refresh
			break
		} else {
			sleep 1
		}
	}		
}
block Mass_Zone_Change_Anti_Lock {
	times 120 {
		if loading {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "All clients are loading..."
			}
			sleep 2
		} else {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Waiting 2 seconds then continuing..."
			}
			sleep 2
			break
		}
	}
}
block Check_X_Key_Type_For_P1 {
	times 30 {
		if p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground'] {
			if p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground', 'TeamUpButton'] {
				if NOT playercount 1 {
					if NOT p1:p2 sameplace {
						call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P1
					}
					if NOT sameplace {
						call Waiting_For_All_To_Arrive
						return
					} else {
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "All clients at sigil. Pressing X on all clients..."
							print "Entering dungeon, reseting quest check state..."
						}
						var QuestCheckState = 0
						times 15 {
							sendkey X, .1
						}
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "Waiting manual 15 seconds before resuming..."
						}
						sleep 15
						call Mass_Zone_Change_Anti_Lock
						if NOT sameplace {
							if DebugMode = True {
								print "All clients are not in the dungeon. Backing out..."
							}
							times 2 {
								sendkey S, 1
							}
							sleep 2
							call Close_Menus
						}
					}
				} else {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Pressing X..."
						print "Entering dungeon, reseting quest check state..."
					}
					var QuestCheckState = 0
					times 15 {
						sendkey X, .1
					}
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Manually waiting 15 seconds..."
					}
					sleep 15
				}
			} else {
				sleep 1
				if NOT sameplace {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Pressing X on P1..."
					}
					times 20 {
						p1 sendkey X, .1
						if p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton'] {
							times 10 {
								p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton']
							}
						}
					}
				} else {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Dialog detected. Pressing X on all clients..."
					}
					times 20 {
						sendkey X, .1
						if windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton'] {
							times 10 {
								clickwindow ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton']
							}
						}
					}
				}
				call Clear_Dialogue
				break
			}
		}
		sleep .1
	}
}

					}
					var QuestCheckState = 0
					times 15 {
						sendkey X, .1
					}
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Manually waiting 15 seconds..."
					}
					sleep 15
				}
			} else {
				sleep 1
				if NOT sameplace {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Pressing X on P3..."
					}
					times 20 {
						p3 sendkey X, .1
						if p3 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton'] {
							times 10 {
								p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton']
							}
						}
					}
				} else {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Dialog detected. Pressing X on all clients..."
					}
					times 20 {
						sendkey X, .1
						if windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton'] {
							times 10 {
								clickwindow ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton']
							}
						}
					}
				}
				call Clear_Dialogue
				break
			}
		}
		sleep .1
	}
}
block Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4 {
	times 30 {
		if p4 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground'] {
			if p4 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground', 'TeamUpButton'] {
				if NOT playercount 1 {
					if NOT p1:p4 sameplace {
						call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P4
					}
					if NOT sameplace {
						call Waiting_For_All_To_Arrive
						return
					} else {
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "All clients at sigil. Pressing X on all clients..."
						}
						times 15 {
							sendkey X, .1
						}
						var QuestCheckState = 0
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "Waiting manual 15 seconds before resuming..."
							print "Entering dungeon, reseting quest check state..."
						}
						sleep 15
						call Mass_Zone_Change_Anti_Lock
						if NOT sameplace {
							if DebugMode = True {
								print "All clients are not in the dungeon. Backing out..."
							}
							times 2 {
								sendkey S, 1
							}
							sleep 2
							call Close_Menus
						}
					}
				} else {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Pressing X..."
						print "Entering dungeon, reseting quest check state..."
					}
					var QuestCheckState = 0
					times 15 {
						sendkey X, .1
					}
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Manually waiting 15 seconds..."
					}
					sleep 15
				}
			} else {
				sleep 1
				if NOT sameplace {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Pressing X on P4..."
					}
					times 20 {
						p4 sendkey X, .1
						if p4 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton'] {
							times 10 {
								p4 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton']
							}
						}
					}
				} else {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "Dialog detected. Pressing X on all clients..."
					}
					times 20 {
						sendkey X, .1
						if windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton'] {
							times 10 {
								clickwindow ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'ControlSprite', 'optionsLayout', 'NPCServicesOptionWindow', 'OptionButton']
							}
						}
					}
				}
				call Clear_Dialogue
				break
			}
		}
		sleep .1
	}
}

block Dungeons_And_Broken_Quests_For_P2 {
	if p2 tracking_quest "fall of pompeii" {
		if p2 tracking_goal "find petrified wood in tartarus" {
			if p2 inzone Aquila/AQ_Z03_Tartarus {
				times 100 {
					tp XYZ(-1183.318, -2830.451, 67.223)
					entitytp petrified
					times 30 {
						sendkey X, .1
					}
					if NOT p2 tracking_goal "find petrified wood in tartarus" {
						break
					}
				}
			}
		} elif p2 tracking_goal "repair urn in tartarus" {
			if p2 inzone Aquila/Interiors/AQ_Z03_TempleOfLonging {
				times 10 {

						}
					}
					tp XYZ(913.483, 887.619, 60.893)
					times 30 {
						sendkey X, .1
					}
					tp XYZ(854.758, -876.959, 38.828)
					times 30 {
						sendkey X, .1
					}
					tp XYZ(-929.005, -912.058, 60.903)
					times 30 {
						sendkey X, .1

			p4 tp XYZ(-12903.21875, -5954.9208984375, -348.5147705078125)
			sleep 10
		} 
		if p4 tracking_goal "Go To Ormund the Surly in Vestrilund" {
			p4 tp XYZ(-10214.9951171875, -4718.0244140625, -348.5156555175781)
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4
		} 
		if p4 tracking_goal "Go To Osvud Fleetpaw in Vestrilund" {
			p4 tp quest
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4
		}
	}     
	#__________ Corrected Quests in Sudrilund
	if p4 inzone Grizzleheim/GH_HFjord/GH_Sudrilund {
		while p4 tracking_goal "Collect Wild Hemlock  in Sudrilund" {
			p4 tp XYZ(3988.144775390625, 5997.1259765625, -468.0919494628906)
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4
			p4 tp XYZ(7446.3369140625, -4992.1962890625, 78.12631225585938)
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4
			p4 tp XYZ(-1893.305908203125, -7496.037109375, 1035.1907958984375) 
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4
		}
		if p4 tracking_goal "Use Thyra's Cauldron in Sudrilund" {
			p4 tp XYZ(1620.2459716796875, -8564.72265625, 1065.052001953125)
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4
		} 
		if p4 tracking_goal "Use Urn in Sudrilund" {
			p4 tp XYZ(-775.4154663085938, 1116.015625, 1408.4415283203125)
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4
		}  
	} 
}
######################################################################################################
block Extreme_Low_Health_Check {
	if playercount 2 {
		if p1 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p1 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p1 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p2 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p2 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p2 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}		
	} elif playercount 3 {
		if p1 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p1 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p1 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p2 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p2 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed

					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p3 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p3 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p3 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p3 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
	} elif playercount 4 {
		if p1 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p1 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p1 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p2 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p2 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p2 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p3 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p3 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p3 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p3 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p4 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p4 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p4 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p4 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
	} else {
		if healthbelow 10% {
			usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
block Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat {
	block Check_If_Died {
		if playercount 2 {
			if p1 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p1 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if Delay_Combat = True {
					until p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'QuitButton'] {
						p2 sendkey ESC, .5
					}
				}
				if p1 incombat {
					until NOT p1 incombat {
						p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					if Delay_Combat = True {
						until NOT p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'QuitButton'] {
							p2 sendkey ESC, .5
						}
					}
					return
				}
				until p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p2 healthbelow 1% {
				if p2 incombat {
					until NOT p2 incombat {
						p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p2 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Return_To_Booster_P2
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
		} elif playercount 3 {
			if p1 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p1 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if p1 incombat {
					until NOT p1 incombat {
						p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P3
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p2 healthbelow 1% {
				if p2 incombat {
					until NOT p2 incombat {
						p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p2 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Return_To_Booster_P2
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p3 healthbelow 1% {
				if p3 incombat {
					until NOT p3 incombat {
						p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p3 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Return_To_Booster_P3
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
		} elif playercount 4 {
			if p1 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p1 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if p1 incombat {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p1 died but still in combat. Fleeing..."
					}
					until NOT p1 incombat {
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "Clicking Flee button to exit combat after death..."
						}
						p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P1 teleporting to help P4 after death..."
				}
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P4
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p2 healthbelow 1% {
				if p2 incombat {
					until NOT p2 incombat {
						p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p2 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Return_To_Booster_P2
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p3 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p3 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if p3 incombat {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p3 died but still in combat. Fleeing..."
					}
					until NOT p3 incombat {
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "Clicking Flee button to exit combat after death..."
						}
						p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p3 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P3 returning to P1 after death recovery..."
				}
				call Return_To_Booster_P3
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p4 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p4 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if p4 incombat {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p4 died but still in combat. Fleeing..."
					}
					until NOT p4 incombat {
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "Clicking Flee button to exit combat after death..."
						}
						p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p4 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p4 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P4 returning to P1 after death recovery..."
				}
				call Return_To_Booster_P4
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
		}
	}		
	block All_Questing_Combat_Check {
		if All_Accounts_Are_Questing = True {
			print "All clients questing. Checking combat..."
			if p1 incombat {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P1 is in combat. Attempting to bring other clients into combat..."
				}
				times 100 {
					if NOT incombat {
						tp XYZ(0,0,100000)
						tp p1
					} else {
						print "All clients in combat."
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			} elif p2 incombat {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P2 is in combat. Attempting to bring other clients into combat..."
				}
				times 100 {
					if NOT incombat {
						tp XYZ(0,0,100000)
						tp p2
					} else {
						print "All clients in combat."
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			} elif p3 incombat {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P3 is in combat. Attempting to bring other clients into combat..."
				}
				times 100 {
					if NOT incombat {
						tp XYZ(0,0,100000)
						tp p3
					} else {
						print "All clients in combat."
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			} elif p4 incombat {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P4 is in combat. Attempting to bring other clients into combat..."
				}
				times 100 {
					if NOT incombat {
						tp XYZ(0,0,100000)
						tp p4
					} else {
						print "All clients in combat."
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	block Refresh_Health {
		times 4 {
			entitytp health
			entitytp mana
		}
		tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
		call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
	}
	if playercount 1 {
		if p1 incombat {
			p1 waitforcombat completion
			times 4 {
				entitytp health
				entitytp mana
			}
			tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
			call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
		}
	} elif playercount 2 {
		call All_Questing_Combat_Check
		if p2 incombat {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is in combat. P1 going to help..."
			}
			if samezone and NOT except p2 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p2 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p2
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p2 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p2
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p2 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p2
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
	} elif playercount 3 {
		call All_Questing_Combat_Check
		if p2 incombat {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is in combat. Attempting to bring clients to help..."
			}
			if samezone and NOT except p2 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p2 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p2
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p2 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p2
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p2 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p2
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
		if p3 incombat {
			if samezone and NOT except p3 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p3 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p3
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p3 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P3
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p3
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p3 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p3
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
	} elif playercount 4 {
		call All_Questing_Combat_Check
		if p2 incombat {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is in combat. Attempting to bring clients to help..."
			}
			if samezone and NOT except p2 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p2 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p2
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p2 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p2
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p2 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p2
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
		if p3 incombat {
			if samezone and NOT except p3 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p3 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p3
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p3 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P3
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p3
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p3 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p3
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
		if p4 incombat {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p4 is in combat. P1 going to help..."
			}
			if samezone and NOT except p4 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p4 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p4
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p4 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P4
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p4
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p4 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p4
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
	}
}
block Debug_Quest_Checker {
	if DebugMode = True { 
		if any hasdialogue {
			times 10 {
				sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
			}
		}
		if playercount 1 {
			if hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			logquest
			if hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			loggoal
			if hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			logzone
			if hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
		} else {
			except p1 logquest
			if any hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			except p1 loggoal
			if any hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			except p1 logzone
			if any hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
block Check_If_On_Char_Screen {
	if Recconect_To_Game = True {
		if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'mainWindow', 'btnPlay'] and any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'mainWindow', 'btnNew'] {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print anyclientlist
				print "Client(s) in character selection. Attempting to press Play..."
			}
			times 3 {
				sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'mainWindow', 'btnPlay']
			}
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Pressed Play. Waiting for loading..."
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT sameany windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowQuickChat', 'btnHelpChat'] {
					sleep .1
				} else {
					sleep 2 # Additional 2 second sleep for extra safety
					return
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
block Check_If_Paused {
	if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'DeckConfigurationWindow', 'ControlSprite', 'DeckPage'] {
		print "Pausing auto-quester. To resume, press the key P or get off the spell deck page."
		times 1000 {
			if NOT hasxyz XYZ(0,100000,0) {
				tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
			} else {
				break
			}
			sleep .1
		}
		loop {
			if NOT sameany windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'DeckConfigurationWindow', 'ControlSprite', 'DeckPage'] {
				print "Resuming..."
				break
			}
			sendkey A, 1
		}
	}
}
block Watchdog_Check {
	# Detects if the bot is stuck by monitoring zone changes.
	# If any player changes zones, the bot is progressing - reset the counter.
	# If no zone change occurs over many iterations, trigger recovery.
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Watchdog: Checking for zone changes..."
	}
	if any zonechanged {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Watchdog: Zone change detected. Resetting watchdog."
		}
		var WatchdogState = 0
		return
	}
	# No zone change - advance the state machine
	if WatchdogState = 0 {
		var WatchdogState = 1
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 1 {
		var WatchdogState = 2
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 2 {
		var WatchdogState = 3
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 3 {
		var WatchdogState = 4
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 4 {
		var WatchdogState = 5
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 5 {
		var WatchdogState = 6
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 6 {
		var WatchdogState = 7
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 7 {
		var WatchdogState = 8
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 8 {
		var WatchdogState = 9
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 9 {
		var WatchdogState = 10
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 10 {
		var WatchdogState = 11
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 11 {
		var WatchdogState = 12
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 12 {
		var WatchdogState = 13
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 13 {
		var WatchdogState = 14
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 14 {
		var WatchdogState = 15
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 15 {
		var WatchdogState = 16
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 16 {
		var WatchdogState = 17
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 17 {
		var WatchdogState = 18
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 18 {
		var WatchdogState = 19
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 19 {
		var WatchdogState = 20
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 20 {
		var WatchdogState = 21
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 21 {
		var WatchdogState = 22
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 22 {
		var WatchdogState = 23
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 23 {
		var WatchdogState = 24
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 24 {
		var WatchdogState = 25
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 25 {
		var WatchdogState = 26
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 26 {
		var WatchdogState = 27
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 27 {
		var WatchdogState = 28
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 28 {
		var WatchdogState = 29
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 29 {
		print "WATCHDOG TRIGGERED: No zone change detected. Initiating recovery..."
		call Close_Menus
		times 3 {
			tp XYZ(0,1000000,0)
		}
		times 3 {
			clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'GoHomeButton'] 
		}
		print "Sleeping 30 seconds... The bot is not broken."
		sleep 30
		var WatchdogState = 0
		print "Watchdog: Recovery complete. Resuming normal operation."
		return
	}
}
block Orient_Edge_Cases {
	if any windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "sister constance" {
		if sameany inzone Avalon/AV_Z07_OuterYard {
			sameany turncam 2.11 
		} 
	}
	if any windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "warwick" {
		if sameany inzone Avalon/AV_Z07_OuterYard {
			sameany turncam 5.93
		} 
	}
	if any windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "merle ambrose" {
		if sameany inzone WizardCity/Interiors/WC_Headmistress_House {
			sameany turncam 3.35
		} 
	}
}
